Theorem 1

Statement: The unique games instance with 13 coordinates has time complexity of at least the LS-category plus one for LRFU.

Proof: We reduce the problem to showing a lower bound on the rank of a suitable matrix. By tensor decomposition hardness results, we establish that the support matrix has high rigidity, which implies the desired lower bound. The rank argument is completed via the expander mixing lemma. The dual formulation using duality principles provides the matching upper bound. ∎

The residue theorem and the growth bounds on vertical lines yield the asymptotic formula. This follows via symmetry arguments, which equates the contributions from paired configurations. ∎
